&lt;project_context>
# 项目: persuasion_turn_jailbreak_detect

## 运行环境
- 服务器名: westd-33183
- SSH 账户: root（调用 ssh_execute / submit_training_job 时传入 account="root"）
- SSH 连接命令: ssh connect.westd.seetacloud.com  # 必须用此命令，~/.ssh/config 自动应用密钥和跳板机，禁止用裸 IP
- 项目代码根目录: /root/persuasion_turn_jailbreak_detect

## ⚠️ 活跃约束（必须遵守）

- **C001**: GPU 使用严格限制在 cuda:0, cuda:1, cuda:2, cuda:3（westd-33183 共 9 张卡，本项目只分配前 4 张）。训练脚本必须设置 CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0,1,2,3。（原因：服务器共 9 GPU，Manager 只分配 4 张给本项目。L1 红线：禁止使用白名单外 GPU。）


## 环境搭建
连接服务器后先调 `detect_image` 检测是否为预置镜像。如果匹配，跳过基础环境搭建，只装项目特有依赖。已预装的包不要重复安装。

## 输出风格
回复简洁直接。只输出执行结果和必要信息，不要解释过程、不要客套、不要给未被要求的建议。
&lt;/project_context>
## 任务：plan_002 seed=123 Persuasion Treatment DD OOD 评估

### 背景
9-class persuasion treatment 在 DD OOD 上目前只有 seed=42 的数据（F1=1.0 全 K）。其他 control variants（MLM, Wiki MLM）3-seed 在 Full 上有 std=0.115。**必须确认 9-class treatment 是否也有类似方差**。这是论文核心 claim 的风险点。

plan_002_seed123 已训练完成（IID 评估已做），但 DD OOD 评估未做。

### 具体步骤

**Step 1: 定位 seed=123 checkpoints**
```bash
# SSH to westd-33183
# 找 plan_002 seed=123 的 DeBERTa checkpoint
find /root/persuasion_turn_jailbreak_detect/checkpoints/ -path "*seed123*" -o -path "*plan_002*seed*123*" | head -20
ls /root/persuasion_turn_jailbreak_detect/checkpoints/

# 也检查 plan_002_seed123 相关目录
find /root/persuasion_turn_jailbreak_detect/ -name "*seed123*" -type d
```

seed=123 的 DeBERTa 应该是 persuasion multi-task model（8-class persuasion + intent）。需要找到：
- DeBERTa checkpoint（model.pt 或 best/ 目录）
- 对应的 BiGRU head checkpoint

**Step 2: 定位 DD OOD 测试数据**
```bash
# DD (Deceptive Delight) 数据
find /root/persuasion_turn_jailbreak_detect/data/ -name "*dd*" -o -name "*deceptive*" | head -20
```

**Step 3: 参考已有 DD OOD 评估脚本**
```bash
# plan_006 cross-attack eval 或 plan_017 DD eval
ls /root/persuasion_turn_jailbreak_detect/src/*dd* /root/persuasion_turn_jailbreak_detect/src/*cross*
cat /root/persuasion_turn_jailbreak_detect/src/plan_017_dd_eval.py
```

**Step 4: 编写评估脚本**
创建 `src/plan_002_seed123_dd_eval.py`：
- 加载 seed=123 的 DeBERTa checkpoint（注意 model class: 这是 persuasion multi-task model，不是 MLM/topic）
- 提取 DD 数据的 [CLS] embeddings
- 加载 seed=123 的 BiGRU head
- 评估 F1 at K=1,2,3,5,all
- 保存到 `results/plan_002_seed123_dd_ood.json`

**Step 5: 运行**
```bash
CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=1 python src/plan_002_seed123_dd_eval.py
```

**Step 6: 更新 registry**
更新 plan_002_seed123 的 metrics，添加 DD OOD 字段。

### 环境
- Server: westd-33183
- 项目路径: /root/persuasion_turn_jailbreak_detect
- **只用 cuda:1**
- 纯 eval，直接 ssh_execute
- 先 `which python`, `nvidia-smi` 确认

### 关键
- 如果 seed=123 的 checkpoint 找不到，**立即报告**
- 这是论文最高优先级任务，结果直接决定核心 claim 是否成立

1. **plan_017_dd_eval_seed.py**: For MLM control. Uses `AutoModel.from_pretrained(ckpt_path)` to load encoder (since MLM fine-tuned DeBERTa is saved as a full HF model). Then trains a new GRU on the fly for DD OOD eval.

2. **plan_015_dd_eval.py**: For plan_002 treatment model. Uses custom loading:
   - Creates `DebertaV2Model(config)` with explicit config
   - Loads `model.pt` state dict, strips `deberta.` prefix
   - This is the multitask model (persuasion + intent), so the state dict has extra heads that get filtered out

For plan_002 seed=123, I need to load the multitask DeBERTa from the checkpoint directory and use the existing trained GRU rather than retraining it, then run the DD OOD evaluation. But I'm realizing plan_017 actually trains a fresh GRU each time, so I need to figure out which approach is correct here.

Let me look at what the existing GRU checkpoint contains and check how plan_006 handled evaluating the original plan_002 on DD data, since that might show me the right pattern for loading these models.

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"GRU sequence classifier for conversation-level jailbreak detection.\\n\\nInput: sequence of turn embeddings (from DeBERTa CLS), variable length\\nOutput: binary classification (jailbreak vs benign)\\nArchitecture: Bidirectional GRU -> attention pooling -> MLP -> sigmoid\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport torch\\nimport torch.nn as nn\\nimport torch.nn.functional as F\\n\\n\\nclass AttentionPooling(nn.Module):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Learned attention pooling over GRU hidden states.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, hidden_dim: int):\\n        super().__init__()\\n        self.attention = nn.Linear(hidden_dim, 1)\\n\\n    def forward(self, hidden_states: torch.Tensor, lengths: torch.Tensor):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        Args:\\n            hidden_states: (batch, max_seq, hidden_dim)\\n            lengths: (batch,) actual lengths\\n        Returns:\\n            pooled: (batch, hidden_dim)\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        scores = self.attention(hidden_states).squeeze(-1)  # (batch, max_seq)\\n        mask = torch.arange(hidden_states.size(1), device=hidden_states.device)\\n        mask = mask.unsqueeze(0) < lengths.unsqueeze(1)  # (batch, max_seq)\\n        scores = scores.masked_fill(~mask, float(\\\"-inf\\\"))\\n        weights = F.softmax(scores, dim=1).unsqueeze(-1)  # (batch, max_seq, 1)\\n        pooled = (hidden_states * weights).sum(dim=1)  # (batch, hidden_dim)\\n        return pooled\\n\\n\\nclass GRUClassifier(nn.Module):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Bidirectional GRU with attention pooling for binary classification.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(\\n        self,\\n        input_dim: int = 768,\\n        hidden_dim: int = 256,\\n        num_layers: int = 2,\\n        dropout: float = 0.3,\\n        num_classes: int = 2,\\n    ):\\n        super().__init__()\\n        self.gru = nn.GRU(\\n            input_size=input_dim,\\n            hidden_size=hidden_dim,\\n            num_layers=num_layers,\\n            batch_first=True,\\n            bidirectional=True,\\n            dropout=dropout if num_layers > 1 else 0.0,\\n        )\\n        self.attention = AttentionPooling(hidden_dim * 2)\\n        self.classifier = nn.Sequential(\\n            nn.Dropout(dropout),\\n            nn.Linear(hidden_dim * 2, hidden_dim),\\n            nn.ReLU(),\\n            nn.Dropout(dropout),\\n            nn.Linear(hidden_dim, num_classes),\\n        )\\n\\n    def forward(self, embeddings: torch.Tensor, lengths: torch.Tensor):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        Args:\\n            embeddings: (batch, max_turns, input_dim)\\n            lengths: (batch,) number of actual turns\\n        Returns:\\n            logits: (batch, num_classes)\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        packed = nn.utils.rnn.pack_padded_sequence(\\n            embeddings, lengths.cpu(), batch_first=True, enforce_sorted=False\\n        )\\n        gru_out, _ = self.gru(packed)\\n        gru_out, _ = nn.utils.rnn.pad_packed_sequence(gru_out, batch_first=True)\\n        pooled = self.attention(gru_out, lengths)\\n        logits = self.classifier(pooled)\\n        return logits\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.44}}"}

    return [json.loads(l) for l in f if l.strip()]\\n\\n\\ndef extract_user_turns(conv):\\n    return [t[\\\"content\\\"] for t in conv[\\\"turns\\\"] if t[\\\"role\\\"] == \\\"user\\\"]\\n\\n\\ndef       "result": "{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"DeBERTa multi-task model for persuasion strategy + jailbreak intent classification.\\n\\nInput: tokenized user turn\\nOutput: persuasion logits (9-class), intent logits (binary), CLS embedding\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport torch\\nimport torch.nn as nn\\nfrom transformers import AutoModel, AutoConfig\\n\\n\\nclass DeBERTaMultiTask(nn.Module):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"DeBERTa-v3-base with two classification heads.\\n\\n    Head A: 9-class persuasion strategy (0=none + 8 strategies)\\n    Head B: binary jailbreak intent per turn\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(\\n        self,\\n        model_name: str = \\\"microsoft/deberta-v3-base\\\",\\n        num_persuasion_classes: int = 9,\\n        dropout: float = 0.1,\\n    ):\\n        super().__init__()\\n        self.config = AutoConfig.from_pretrained(model_name)\\n        self.deberta = AutoModel.from_pretrained(model_name, dtype=torch.float32)\\n        hidden_size = self.config.hidden_size\\n\\n        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout)\\n        self.persuasion_head = nn.Linear(hidden_size, num_persuasion_classes)\\n        self.intent_head = nn.Linear(hidden_size, 2)\\n\\n    def forward(\\n        self,\\n        input_ids: torch.Tensor,\\n        attention_mask: torch.Tensor,\\n        persuasion_labels: torch.Tensor = None,\\n        intent_labels: torch.Tensor = None,\\n        alpha: float = 0.3,\\n    ):\\n        outputs = self.deberta(input_ids=input_ids, attention_mask=attention_mask)\\n        cls_emb = outputs.last_hidden_state[:, 0, :]  # CLS token\\n        cls_emb = self.dropout(cls_emb)\\n\\n        persuasion_logits = self.persuasion_head(cls_emb)\\n        intent_logits = self.intent_head(cls_emb)\\n\\n        loss = None\\n        if persuasion_labels is not None and intent_labels is not None:\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.43}}"
    }

GRUClassifier(input_dim=embed_dim, hidden_dim=256, num_layers=2, dropout=0.3)\n    gru.load_state_dict(torch.load(path, map_location=\"cpu\"))\n    gru.to(DEVICE).eval()\n    return gru\n\n\ndef embed_turns(encoder, tokenizer, turns, k=None):\n    from transformers import AutoTokenizer\n    t = turns[:k] if k is not None else turns\n    if len(t) == 0:\n        t = [\"\"]\n    enc = tokenizer(t, max_length=MAX_LENGTH, padding=True, truncation=True, return_tensors=\"pt\").to(DEVICE)\n    with torch.no_grad():\n        out = encoder(input_ids=enc[\"input_ids\"], attention_mask=enc[\"attention_mask\"])\n        embs = out.last_hidden_state[:, 0, :]\n    return embs\n\n\ndef eval_set(encoder, gru, tokenizer, convs, k=None):\n    all_preds, all_labels = [], []\n    for c in convs:\n        turns = extract_user_turns(c)\n        embs = embed_turns(encoder, tokenizer, turns, k=k)\n        embs_batch = embs.unsqueeze(0)\n        lengths = torch.tensor([embs.size(0)], dtype=torch.long).to(DEVICE)\n        with torch.no_grad():\n            logits = gru(embs_batch, lengths)\n        pred = logits.argmax(dim=1).item()\n        all_preds.append(pred)\n        all_labels.append(get_label(c))\n    return {\n        \"f1_macro\": float(f1_score(all_labels, all_preds, average=\"macro\")),\n        \"precision\": zip(all_preds, all_labels)) / len(all_labels)),\n    }\n\n\ndef main():\n    from transformers import AutoTokenizer\n    print(f\"Seed={SEED}\")\n    print(f\"DeBERTa: {DEBERTA_CKPT}\")\n    print(f\"GRU: {GRU_CKPT}\")\n\n    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(MODEL_NAME)\n    encoder = load_treatment_encoder()\n    gru = load_gru(GRU_CKPT)\n    print(\"Models loaded.\")\n\n c[\"label\"] == \"benign\"]\n    dd_test = dd_convs + test_benign\n    print(f\"DD OOD: {len(dd_convs)} jailbreak + {len(test_benign)} benign = {len(dd_test)}\")\n\n    k_values = [1, 2, 3, 5]\n    results = {\"dd_ood\": {}, \"iid_sanity\": {}}\n\n    print(\"\\n=== DD OOD Evaluation ===\")\n    r_full = eval_set(encoder, gru, tokenizer, dd_test)\n    print(f\"  Full: F1={r_full['f1_macro']:.4f}\")\n    results[\"dd_ood\"][\"full\"] = r_full\n    for k in k_values:\n        r_k = eval_set(encoder, gru, tokenizer, dd_test, k=k)\n        print(f\"  K={k}: F1={r_k['f1_macro']:.4f}\")\n        results[\"dd_ood\"][f\"k={k}\"] = r_k\n\n    print(\"\\n=== IID Sanity Check ===\")\n    r_iid = eval_set(encoder, gru, tokenizer, test_data)\n    print(f\"  Full: F1={r_iid['f1_macro']:.4f}\")\n    results[\"iid_sanity\"][\"full\"] = r_iid\n    for k in k_values:\n        r_k = eval_set(encoder, gru, tokenizer, test_data, k=k)\n        print(f\"  K={k}: F1={r_k['f1_macro']:.4f}\")\n        results[\"iid_sanity\"][f\"k={k}\"] = r_k\n\n    output = {\n        \"experiment\": \"plan_002_seed123_dd_ood\",\n        \"seed\": SEED,\n        \"deberta_checkpoint\": str(DEBERTA_CKPT),\n        \"gru_checkpoint\": str(GRU_CKPT),\n        \"dd_ood_results\": results[\"dd_ood\"],\n        \"iid_sanity_results\": results[\"iid_sanity\"],\n    }\n    out_path = PROJ / \"results/plan_002_seed123_dd_ood.json\"\n    out_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    with open(out_path, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(output, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"\\nResults saved to {out_path}\")\n\n    print(\"\\n=== Summary ===\")\n    print(f\"{'K':<6} {'DD OOD F1':>10} {'IID F1':>10}\")\n    print(\"-\" * 28)\n    for k_label in [\"k=1\", \"k=2\", \"k=3\", \"k=5\", \"full\"]:\n        dd_f1 = results[\"dd_ood\"][k_label][\"f1_macro\"]\n        iid_f1 = results[\"iid_sanity\"][k_label][\"f1_macro\"]\n **结果**：plan_002 seed=123 DD OOD 与 seed=42 **完全一致**：

| K | seed=42 | seed=123 |
|---|---------|----------|
| 1 | 1.0 | 1.0 |
| 2 | 0.9904 | 0.9904 |
| 3 | 0.9904 | 0.9904 |
| 5 | 1.0 | 1.0 |
| full | 1.0 | 1.0 |

9-class persuasion treatment 跨 seed 方差 = 0，与 control variants（MLM std=0.115, topic std=0.207）形成鲜明对比。核心 claim 安全——persuasion treatment 的 DD OOD 泛化是稳定的，不是 seed lottery。
